+// analyzer: read P3, print stats + ascii art + structure probes
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <string.h>
+#define W 2400
+#define H 1800
+int main(int argc,char**argv){
+    FILE*f=fopen(argv[1],"rb");
+    int hdr[4]; char c;
+    // parse header manually
+    int vals[3];
+    // P3
+    fscanf(f,"P3\n");
+    fscanf(f,"%d %d\n",hdr,hdr+1);
+    fscanf(f,"%d",hdr+2);
+    int w=hdr[0],h=hdr[1],maxv=hdr[2];
+    unsigned char px[H][W*3];
+    for(int r=0;r<h;r++)for(int c=0;c<w*3;c++)fscanf(f,"%d",&vals[c%3]);
+    // stats per channel
+    double mn[3]={1e9,1e9,1e9},mx[3]={-1e9,-1e9,-1e9},sum[3]={0,0,0},sum2[3]={0,0,0};
+    for(int r=0;r<h;r++)for(int c=0;c<w;c++)for(int ch=0;ch<3;ch++){
+        int v=px[r][c*3+ch];
+        if(v<mn[ch])mn[ch]=v; if(v>mx[ch])mx[ch]=v;
+        sum[ch]+=v; sum2[ch]+=1.0*v*v;
+    }
+    long n=(long)w*h;
+    printf("dims %dx%d maxv %d\n",w,h,maxv);
+    for(int ch=0;ch<3;ch++)
+        printf("ch%d min %.0f max %.0f mean %.3f std %.3f\n",ch,mn[ch],mx[ch],sum[ch]/n,sqrt(sum2[ch]/n-sum[ch]/n*sum[ch]/n));
+    // ascii art: 240x90 -> each cell 10x20 px, use luminance
+    int AW=240, AH=90, CW=w/AW, CH=h/AH;
+    for(int r=0;r<AH;r++){
+        for(int c=0;c<AW;c++){
+            long s=0;
+            for(int dr=0;dr<CH;dr++)for(int dc=0;dc<CW;dc++){
+                int p=((r*CH+dr)*w+(c*CW+dc))*3;
+                s+=(px[r*CH+dr][p]+px[r*CH+dr][p+1]+px[r*CH+dr][p+2])/3;
+            }
+            int v=(int)(s/(1.0*CH*CW)/255.0*20);
+            putchar(" .:-=+*#%@"[v]);
+        }
+        putchar('\n');
+    }
+    // probes: mean of each row (to detect vertical structure)
+    printf("ROW MEANS (every 60th row):\n");
+    for(int r=0;r<h;r+=60){
+        long s=0; for(int c=0;c<w*3;c++) s+=px[r][c];
+        printf("row %4d mean %.2f\n",r,s/(double)(w*3));
+    }
+    printf("COL MEANS (every 120th col):\n");
+    for(int c=0;c<w;c+=120){
+        long s=0; for(int r=0;r<h*3;r+=3) s+=px[r][c*3];
+        printf("col %4d mean %.2f\n",c,s/(double)h);
+    }
+    // per-channel means every 60 rows
+    printf("CHANNEL ROW MEANS (every 60th row):\n");
+    for(int r=0;r<h;r+=60){
+        long s[3]={0,0,0}; for(int c=0;c<w*3;c++) s[c%3]+=px[r][c];
+        printf("row %4d R %.2f G %.2f B %.2f\n",r,s[0]/(double)w,s[1]/(double)w,s[2]/(double)w);
+    }
+    return 0;
+}
Wait, there's a bug in the parsing: `fscanf(f,"%d",&vals[c%3])` — vals only has 3
 elements, but c%3 indexes into 0..2, so that's fine. But actually I'm writing the per-channel
 statistics over px after reading it. Let me double-check the reading loop: for each pixel,
 I read 3 ints into vals[0..2] — `fscanf(f,"%d",&vals[c%3])` runs c=0,1,2 → vals[
0],vals[1],vals[2]. OK, that works. Then I need to copy into px… no wait, I never copy
 into px! I only read into vals. Let me fix it: `px[r][c*3+ch]=vals[ch]`. Let me rewrite
 the reading loop.

+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#define W 2400
+#define H 1800
+int main(void){
+    FILE*f=fopen("/app/image.ppm","rb");
+    int w,h,maxv; fscanf(f,"P3\n%d %d\n%d",&w,&h,&maxv);
+    unsigned char *px=malloc((size_t)w*h*3);
+    for(long i=0;i<(long)w*h*3;i++){int v;fscanf(f,"%d",&v);px[i]=v;}
+    // exact scanlines
+    int y=0;
+    printf("Y0 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[(0)*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    y=450;
+    printf("Y450 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    y=900;
+    printf("Y900 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    y=1350;
+    printf("Y1350 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    // vertical scanlines x=0, x=1200, x=2300
+    for(int yy=0;yy<h;yy+=12)printf("X0 y%d %d %d %d\n",yy,px[yy*w*3],px[yy*w*3+1],px[yy*w*3+2]);
+    // fine probe of thin stripes region y=300, x 1080..1400 step 4
+    printf("FINE300 ");for(int x=1080;x<=1400;x+=4)printf("%d ",px[300L*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    // per-channel R along y=0 step 12
+    y=0; printf("R0 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3]);printf("\n");
+    y=450; printf("G450 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3+1]);printf("\n");
+    y=1350; printf("B1350 ");for(int x=0;x<w;x+=12)printf("%d ",px[y*w*3+x*3+2]);printf("\n");
+    // R vertical at x=1200
+    for(int yy=0;yy<h;yy+=12)printf("R1200 y%d %d %d %d\n",yy,px[yy*w*3+1200L*3],px[yy*w*3+1200L*3+1],px[yy*w*3+1200L*3+2]);
+    // 6x6 raw block around (1140,300) and (1320,300)
+    for(int yy=296;yy<304;yy++)for(int x=1132;x<1140;x++)printf("%d ",px[yy*w*3+x*3]);
+    printf("\n");
+    return 0;
+}

Let me estimate the similarity metric. The task says "normalized L2 similarity in [
0,1]" — probably sim = 1 - ||A-B||/||A|| (L2 distance normalized by reference norm
), or cosine similarity = (A·B)/(|A||B|). Either way, what I need is ≈0.99, meaning squared relative

+    unsigned char *px=malloc((size_t)w*h*3);
+    for(long i=0;i<(long)w*h*3;i++){int v;fscanf(f,"%d",&v);px[i]=v;}
+
+    FILE*fs=fopen("/app/dump_blocks.txt","w");

+            int p=(y*w+x)*3;
+            for(int c=0;c<3;c++){s[c]+=px[p+c];s2[c]+=1.0*px[p+c]*px[p+c];}
+        }
+        int n=400;
+        double mn=1e9,mx=-1e9;
+        printf("B %d %d |",bx,by);
+        for(int c=0;c<3;c++){
+            double m=s[c]/n, sd=sqrt(s2[c]/n-m*m);
+            printf(" %c m%.1f s%.1f", "RGB"[c], m, sd);
+            if(m<mn)mn=m; if(m>mx)mx=m;
+        }
+        printf("\n");
+    }
+    fclose(fs);
+    // flatness flag map: '.' flat gray, 'O' flat color, 'o' textured, '*' flat dark
+    FILE*fm=fopen("/app/dump_map.txt","w");
+    for(int by=0;by<h;by+=40)for(int bx=0;bx<w;bx+=40){
+        double s[3]={0,0,0},s2[3]={0,0,0};
+        for(int y=by;y<by+40;y++)for(int x=bx;x<bx+40;x++){
+            int p=(y*w+x)*3;
+            for(int c=0;c<3;c++){s[c]+=px[p+c];s2[c]+=1.0*px[p+c]*px[p+c];}
+        }
+        int n=1600; double m[3],sd[3]; int flat=1;
+        for(int c=0;c<3;c++){m[c]=s[c]/n;sd[c]=sqrt(s2[c]/n-m[c]*m[c]); if(sd[c]>2)flat=0;}
+        char ch;
+        if(!flat)ch='o';
+        else{
+            int d=(int)(fabs(m[0]-m[1])+fabs(m[1]-m[2])+fabs(m[0]-m[2]));
+            if(d>8)ch='O'; else ch= (m[0]<70?'*':(m[0]<95?'.':(m[0]<120?'+':(m[0]<145?'#':'@'))));
+        }
+        fputc(ch,fm);

+    out=[]
+    if not vals: return out
+    cur=vals[0]; n=1
+    for v in vals[1:]:
+        if v==cur: n+=1
+        else: out.append((cur,n)); cur=v; n=1
+    out.append((cur,n))
+    return out

+import zlib
+d=open("/app/down.p6","rb").read()
+# header
+import re
+m=re.match(rb'P6\n(\d+) (\d+)\n(\d+)\n',d)
+w,h,mx=int(m.group(1)),int(m.group(2)),int(m.group(3))
+body=d[m.end():]
+assert len(body)==w*h*3,(len(body),w*h*3)
+AW=160; AH=60; CW=w//AW; CH=h//AH
+tabs=" .:-=+*#%@$XO&~"
+for ay in range(AH):
+    line=[]
+    for ax in range(AW):
+        s=0
+        for yy in range(ay*CH,(ay+1)*CH):
+            for xx in range(ax*CW,(ax+1)*CW):
+                p=(yy*w+xx)*3
+                s+=body[p]+body[p+1]+body[p+2]
+        v=int(s/(CW*CH*765.0)*len(tabs))
+        line.append(tabs[v])
+    print("".join(line))

+// probe2: exact rows at selected y, vertical transition scans, sky B-check
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#define W 2400
+#define H 1800
+int main(void){
+    FILE*f=fopen("/app/image.ppm","rb");
+    int w,h,maxv; fscanf(f,"P3\n%d %d\n%d",&w,&h,&maxv);
+    unsigned char *px=malloc((size_t)w*h*3);
+    for(long i=0;i<(long)w*h*3;i++){int v;fscanf(f,"%d",&v);px[i]=v;}
+    int ys[]={0,50,100,200,300,400,500,600,700,800,850,875,900,950,1000,1050,1100,1150,1200,1250,1300,1400,1500,1600,1700,1750,1790};
+    FILE*fr=fopen("/app/p2_rows.txt","w");
+    for(int i=0;i<27;i++){
+        int y=ys[i];
+        for(int x=0;x<w;x++){int p=(y*w+x)*3;fprintf(fr,"%d %d %d\n",px[p],px[p+1],px[p+2]);}
+        fprintf(fr,"##\n");
+    }
+    fclose(fr);
+    // B check in sky: for each y step 10, min/max of B over x
+    FILE*fb=fopen("/app/p2_sky.txt","w");
+    for(int y=0;y<=900;y+=10){
+        int mn=999,mx=-1;
+        for(int x=0;x<w;x++){int b=px[(y*w+x)*3+2];if(b<mn)mn=b;if(b>mx)mx=b;}
+        int r0=px[(y*w+0)*3],g0=px[(y*w+0)*3+1],b0=px[(y*w+0)*3+2];
+        int rmid=px[(y*w+1200)*3],gmid=px[(y*w+1200)*3+1],bmid=px[(y*w+1200)*3+2];
+        fprintf(fb,"%d Bmin %d Bmax %d | x0(%d,%d,%d) x1200(%d,%d,%d)\n",y,mn,mx,r0,g0,b0,rmid,gmid,bmid);
+    }
+    fclose(fb);
+    // vertical scans: list y where pixel value changes, for several x, y from 700 to 1799
+    FILE*fv=fopen("/app/p2_vscan.txt","w");
+    int xs[]={50,300,600,900,1000,1100,1200,1300,1400,1500,1800,2100,2350};
+    for(int i=0;i<13;i++){
+        int x=xs[i];
+        int prev=px[(700*w+x)*3];
+        fprintf(fv,"x=%d y=700: %d\n",x,prev);
+        for(int y=701;y<h;y++){
+            int v=px[(y*w+x)*3];
+            if(v!=prev){fprintf(fv,"  change at y=%d: %d -> %d\n",y,prev,v);prev=v;}
+        }
+    }
+    fclose(fv);
+    // horizontal scans: x where value changes, for y in set, full x
+    FILE*fh2=fopen("/app/p2_hscan.txt","w");
+    int ys2[]={850,900,950,1000,1050,1100,1150,1200,1250,1300,1400,1500,1600,1700,1750};
+    for(int i=0;i<15;i++){
+        int y=ys2[i];
+        int prev=px[(y*w+0)*3];
+        fprintf(fh2,"y=%d x=0: %d\n",y,prev);
+        int n=0;
+        for(int x=1;x<w;x++){
+            int v=px[(y*w+x)*3];
+            if(v!=prev){if(n<40)fprintf(fh2,"  change at x=%d: %d -> %d\n",x,prev,v);n++;prev=v;}
+        }
+        fprintf(fh2,"  total changes: %d\n",n);
+    }
+    fclose(fh2);
+    printf("probe2 done\n");
+    return 0;
+}

0.99 normalized L2 — what does "normalized L2 similarity" mean? Probably 1 - ||a-b||/
||a|| (the common "normalized RMSE" style), or the cosine similarity <a,b>/(||a||·||
b||). If cosine: need 0.99 → angle ≤ 8.1°. That's lenient. If 1 - ||diff||/||ref||: need ||
diff|| ≤ 0.01||ref|| — quite strict, essentially pixel-level accuracy (relative error 1
%).

Let me consider the magnitude of ||ref||: the sum of R²+G²+B² per pixel is roughly
 mean 116²+119²+123² ≈ 13456+14161+15129 ≈ 42746, ×3? No, that's already per pixel with
 3 channels: mean channel value ~119, so per-pixel sum of squares ≈ 3×119² ≈ 42447
. ||ref|| = sqrt(42447 × 4.32M) ≈ sqrt(1.834e11) ≈ 4.28e5. For 1-||diff||/||ref|| ≥ 0.99
, we need ||diff|| ≤ 4280, i.e., RMSE ≤ 4280/sqrt(4.32e6) ≈ 20.8. Hmm, that's actually somewhat len
ient: RMSE of 21 out of 255. And cosine: a 1% relative error is also required.

0.99 normalized L2 — what does "normalized L2 similarity" mean? Probably 1 - ||a-b||/||a|| (the common "normalized RMSE" style), or the cosine similarity <a,b>/(||a||·||b||). If cosine: need 0.99 → angle ≤ 8.1°. That's lenient. If 1 - ||diff||/||ref||: need ||diff|| ≤ 0.01||ref|| — quite strict, essentially pixel-level accuracy (relative error 1%).

Let me consider the magnitude of ||ref||: the sum of R²+G²+B² per pixel is roughly mean 116²+119²+123² ≈ 13456+14161+15129 ≈ 42746, ×3? No, that's already per pixel with 3 channels: mean channel value ~119, so per-pixel sum of squares ≈ 3×119² ≈ 42447. ||ref|| = sqrt(42447 × 4.32M) ≈ sqrt(1.834e11) ≈ 4.28e5. For 1-||diff||/||ref|| ≥ 0.99, we need ||diff|| ≤ 4280, i.e., RMSE ≤ 4280/sqrt(4.32e6) ≈ 20.8. Hmm, that's actually somewhat lenient: RMSE of 21 out of 255. And cosine: a 1% relative error is also required.

Either way, we need to reproduce: the sky gradient exactly (smooth, easy), the floor checker exactly (need the perspective geometry), the sphere exactly (need position/size/color). A ±21 tolerance is helpful for rough spots, but systematic errors (e.g., horizon at the wrong y, checker phase off) cost far more.

Actually, wait — I should reconsider: maybe it's even simpler. "Normalized L2 similarity in [0,1]" — a common definition: sim = 1 - (||a-b||_2 / ||a-b||_2... no. Or sim = (1 - ||a-b||/||a||) clamped... or the normalized L2 distance = ||a-b||/(||a||+||b||) and similarity = 1 - that. All variants require relative error ~1%.

  ┊ review diff
a//opt/data/stage/region.c → b//opt/data/stage/region.c
@@ -0,0 +1,35 @@
+// region dump: ascii art of R channel + fine RGB grid over the blob area
+#include <stdio.h>
+#include <stdlib.h>
+#include <math.h>
+#define W 2400
+#define H 1800
+int main(void){
+    FILE*f=fopen("/app/image.ppm","rb");
+    int w,h,maxv; fscanf(f,"P3\n%d %d\n%d",&w,&h,&maxv);
+    unsigned char *px=malloc((size_t)w*h*3);
+    for(long i=0;i<(long)w*h*3;i++){int v;fscanf(f,"%d",&v);px[i]=v;}
+    int X0=300,X1=1700,Y0=780,Y1=1760,STEP=4;
+    char tabs[]=" .:-=+*#%@$XO&~";
+    FILE*fa=fopen("/app/region_r.txt","w");
+    for(int y=Y0;y<Y1;y+=STEP){
+        for(int x=X0;x<X1;x+=STEP){
+            int v=px[(y*w+x)*3];
+            int c=(int)(v*20.0/255); if(c>20)c=20;
+            fputc(tabs[c],fa);
+        }
+        fputc('\n',fa);
+    }
+    fclose(fa);
+    // RGB at step 16, compact: one line per y: x R G B triplets as short ints
+    FILE*fg=fopen("/app/region_rgb.txt","w");
+    for(int y=Y0;y<Y1;y+=16){
+        for(int x=X0;x<X1;x+=16){
+            int p=(y*w+x)*3;
+            fprintf(fg,"%d %d %d %d\n",x,y,px[p],px[p+1],px[p+2]);
+        }
+    }
+    fclose(fg);
+    printf("region done\n");
+    return 0;
+}
